- **science-fair-judge**: Comprehensive science fair project evaluator and judge simulator. Reads a student's poster or presentation (PPTX, PDF, or images), then runs parallel research agents to: (1) verify scientific content accuracy against current literature, (2) evaluate against official judging criteria (ISEF, MSEF, or other fairs), and (3) prepare categorized interview questions with expected strong/weak answers. Outputs a detailed markdown report with scores, feedback, and preparation guidance.
